      const s=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers",uid));
      return s.exists()?Object.assign({uid:s.id},s.data()):null;
    },

    async getLadderTransactions(){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return [];
      const s=await fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderTransactions"));
      const out=[]; s.forEach(d=>{const x=Object.assign({id:d.id},d.data()); x.createdAtMs=cloudSafeTimestampMs(x.createdAt); out.push(x);});
      return out.sort((a,b)=>(b.createdAtMs||0)-(a.createdAtMs||0)).slice(0,200);
    },

    async getTestLadderRanking(){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return [];
      const snap=await fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"testLadderPlayers"));
      const out=[]; snap.forEach(d=>out.push(Object.assign({testPlayerKey:d.id},d.data())));
      return out.sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.seasonPoints||0)-Number(a.seasonPoints||0))||(Number(b.wins||0)-Number(a.wins||0))||String(a.testPlayerKey).localeCompare(String(b.testPlayerKey)));
    },

    async settleTestLadderTournament(code,payload){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!code) return {ok:false,reason:"network"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      const eventCode=String(code).toUpperCase();
      const tournamentSnap=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"tournaments",eventCode));
      if(!tournamentSnap.exists()) return {ok:false,reason:"event-not-found"};
      const tournament=tournamentSnap.data()||{};
      if(tournament.testMode!==true||tournament.eventAuthority!=="test"||tournament.ownerUid!==actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"not-own-test-event",message:"只能結算自己建立的測試賽事"};
      const items=(payload&&payload.participants||[]).filter(x=>x&&x.source==="test"&&/^T\d{3}$/.test(String(x.testPlayerKey||"")));
      if(!items.length) return {ok:true,skipped:true,reason:"no-test-players",message:"本場皆為現場新增選手，未計入 TEST-S1；僅 T001～T064 實驗玩家可累積測試積分",results:[],skippedPlayers:(payload?.participants||[]).map(p=>p.playerName)};
      const results=[]; let newWrites=0;
      for(const item of items){
        const key=String(item.testPlayerKey);
        const txId=eventCode+"_"+key;
        const txRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"testLadderTransactions",txId);
        const playerRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"testLadderPlayers",key);
        const r=await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
          const prior=await tx.get(txRef);
          if(prior.exists()) return {already:true,points:Number(prior.data().delta||0)};
          const ps=await tx.get(playerRef);
          const old=ps.exists()?ps.data():{};
          const delta=Number(item.pointsEarned||0);
          const next={
            testPlayerKey:key, playerName:String(item.playerName||key), seasonId:"TEST-S1",
            seasonPoints:Number(old.seasonPoints||0)+delta, careerPoints:Number(old.careerPoints||0)+delta,
            totalEvents:Number(old.totalEvents||0)+1, wins:Number(old.wins||0)+Number(item.wins||0), losses:Number(old.losses||0)+Number(item.losses||0),
            championCount:Number(old.championCount||0)+(item.placement===1?1:0), runnerUpCount:Number(old.runnerUpCount||0)+(item.placement===2?1:0),
            thirdPlaceCount:Number(old.thirdPlaceCount||0)+(item.placement===3?1:0), fourthPlaceCount:Number(old.fourthPlaceCount||0)+(item.placement===4?1:0),
            updatedAt:Date.now()
          };
          tx.set(playerRef,next,{merge:true});
          tx.set(txRef,{type:"event",eventAuthority:"test",actorUid,testPlayerKey:key,playerName:String(item.playerName||key),seasonId:"TEST-S1",delta,eventCode,eventName:String(payload.eventName||""),placement:item.placement||null,wins:Number(item.wins||0),losses:Number(item.losses||0),createdAt:Date.now()});
          return {already:false,points:delta};
        });
        if(!r.already) newWrites++;
        results.push({testPlayerKey:key,playerName:item.playerName,pointsEarned:r.points,placement:item.placement||null,alreadyAwarded:!!r.already});
      }
      return {ok:true,seasonId:"TEST-S1",awardedAt:Date.now(),alreadyAwarded:newWrites===0,results};
    },

    async settleLadderTournament(code,payload){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!code) return {ok:false,reason:"network"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      if(!window.engagementService||typeof window.engagementService.settleLadder!=="function") throw new Error("service-unavailable");
      return window.engagementService.settleLadder({code:String(code).toUpperCase()});

    },

    async adjustLadderPoints(uid,newSeasonPoints,reason,actorName){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!uid||!reason) return {ok:false,reason:"invalid"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      const playerRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers",uid);
      const controlRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSystem","current");
      const logRef=fx.doc(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderTransactions"));
      return await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
        const ps=await tx.get(playerRef), cs=await tx.get(controlRef);
        if(!ps.exists()) return {ok:false,reason:"not-found"};
        if(!cs.exists()||cs.data().rolloverInProgress===true) return {ok:false,reason:"season-unavailable"};
        const control=cs.data(); const isTrialSeason=String(control.currentSeason||"").toUpperCase()==="S0"||control.isTrialSeason===true;
        const old=ps.data(); const before=Math.max(0,Number(old.seasonPoints||0)); const after=Math.max(0,Number(newSeasonPoints)||0); const delta=after-before;
        const beforeCareer=Math.max(0,Number(old.careerPoints||0)); const afterCareer=isTrialSeason?beforeCareer:Math.max(0,beforeCareer+delta);
        const rankTier=cloudLadderTier(after); const oldHigh=old.highestRankTier||"黑鐵"; const highest=isTrialSeason?oldHigh:(cloudLadderTierIndex(rankTier)>cloudLadderTierIndex(oldHigh)?rankTier:oldHigh);
        tx.update(playerRef,{seasonPoints:after,careerPoints:afterCareer,rankTier,highestRankTier:highest,lastPointsUpdatedAt:fx.serverTimestamp()});
        tx.set(logRef,{type:"adjustment",playerUid:uid,playerName:old.playerName||"",seasonId:old.currentSeason||control.currentSeason||"S1",isTrialSeason,countsTowardCareer:!isTrialSeason,beforeSeasonPoints:before,afterSeasonPoints:after,beforeCareerPoints:beforeCareer,afterCareerPoints:afterCareer,delta,reason,actorUid,actorName:actorName||"",createdAt:fx.serverTimestamp()});
        return {ok:true,before,after,delta};
      });
    },

    async openNewLadderSeason(actorName){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return {ok:false,reason:"network"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      const controlRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSystem","current");

      // Lock the ladder first. Event settlement checks this flag inside its own transaction.
      const lock=await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
        const cs=await tx.get(controlRef); if(!cs.exists()) return {ok:false,reason:"not-initialized"};
        const c=cs.data();
        if(String(c.currentSeason||"").toUpperCase()==="S0") return {ok:false,reason:"s0-auto-cutover",message:"S0 將於 10/1 00:00 自動切換為 S1，不需手動開季"};
        if(c.rolloverInProgress!==true && c.lastRolloverCompletedAtMs && (Date.now()-Number(c.lastRolloverCompletedAtMs)) < 10*60*1000){
          return {ok:true,alreadyCompleted:true,oldSeason:c.lastRolloverFromSeason||null,newSeason:c.currentSeason||"S1"};
        }
        if(c.rolloverInProgress!==true && Number(c.endAt||0) && Date.now()<=Number(c.endAt)){
          return {ok:false,reason:"season-not-ended",endAt:Number(c.endAt)};
        }
        const oldSeason=c.rolloverFromSeason||(c.currentSeason||"S1");
        const newSeason=c.rolloverTargetSeason||cloudNextSeasonId(oldSeason);
        const oldEndAt=Number(c.endAt||0);
        if(c.rolloverInProgress!==true){ tx.update(controlRef,{rolloverInProgress:true,rolloverFromSeason:oldSeason,rolloverTargetSeason:newSeason,updatedAt:fx.serverTimestamp()}); }
        return {ok:true,oldSeason,newSeason,oldEndAt};
      });
      if(!lock.ok) return lock;
      if(lock.alreadyCompleted) return {ok:true,alreadyCompleted:true,oldSeason:lock.oldSeason,newSeason:lock.newSeason};

      const ps=await fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers"));
      const players=ps.docs.map(d=>Object.assign({uid:d.id},d.data()));
      // On retry, players already rolled use their saved old-season final values.
      const finalRows=players.map(p=>{
        const h=p.seasonHistory&&p.seasonHistory[lock.oldSeason];
        return Object.assign({},p,{__finalPoints:h&&h.finalPoints!=null?Number(h.finalPoints):Number(p.seasonPoints||0)});
      }).sort((a,b)=> (b.__finalPoints-a.__finalPoints) || (Number(b.championCount||0)-Number(a.championCount||0)) || (Number(b.runnerUpCount||0)-Number(a.runnerUpCount||0)) || (Number(b.thirdPlaceCount||0)-Number(a.thirdPlaceCount||0)) || (Number(b.fourthPlaceCount||0)-Number(a.fourthPlaceCount||0)) || (Number(b.careerPoints||0)-Number(a.careerPoints||0)) );
      let lastKey=null,lastRank=0;
      finalRows.forEach((p,i)=>{const k=[p.__finalPoints,p.championCount||0,p.runnerUpCount||0,p.thirdPlaceCount||0,p.fourthPlaceCount||0,p.careerPoints||0].join("|"); if(i===0||k!==lastKey)lastRank=i+1; p.__finalRanking=lastRank; lastKey=k;});
      const byUid=Object.fromEntries(finalRows.map(x=>[x.uid,x]));

      const pending=players.filter(p=>p.lastRolloverFromSeason!==lock.oldSeason);
      for(let offset=0;offset<pending.length;offset+=350){
        const batch=fx.writeBatch(dbHandle);
        for(const p of pending.slice(offset,offset+350)){
          const final=byUid[p.uid]; const finalPoints=Number(final.__finalPoints||0); const history=Object.

  {version:"v13.32.2",date:"2026/09/16",timezone:"Asia/Taipei",title:"抽獎活動分享網址與 QR
--- match 2 @ 706961 ---
 admin duplicate-checking) removed.
// This is what gets written to the separate `publicTournaments/{code}`
// document; the full `state` (including registrations) only ever goes to the
// admin/staff-only `tournaments/{code}` document.
function buildPublicTournamentSnapshot(st){
  return Object.assign({}, st, { registrations: [] });
}

async function saveState(){
  state.updatedAt = Date.now();
  if(state.meta && state.meta.registrationEnabled){
    state.meta.registrationStatus = computeRegistrationStatus(state.meta);
  }
  touchCommunityActivity(state);

  // Snapshot NOW. Later UI mutations must not change the payload of this save.
  const snapshot = cloneStateForSave(state);
  const localSavePromise = saveRecord(snapshot);

  if(snapshot.cloudCode && cloudAvailable() && currentRole!=="guest"){
    try{
      const cloudPromise = enqueueCloudStateWrite(snapshot.cloudCode, snapshot);
      const localOk = await localSavePromise;
      const ok = await cloudPromise;
      if(ok){
        cloudSyncPending = false;
        cloudStatus = "connected";
        cloudLastSyncAt = Date.now();
        return localOk ? true : false;
      }
      const lastCode = (typeof window!=="undefined" && window.__BXH_LAST_CLOUD_ERROR_CODE) || "";
      if(lastCode==="permission-denied"){
        cloudAccessLimited = true;
        cloudAccessMessage = "此帳號沒有執行這項雲端寫入的權限。";
        cloudStatus = "connected";
      }else{
        cloudAccessLimited = false; cloudAccessMessage = ""; cloudStatus = "error";
        queueCloudSyncRetry(snapshot.id, snapshot.cloudCode);
      }
      return false;
    }catch(e){
      await localSavePromise;
      const code=(e&&e.code)||"";
      if(code==="permission-denied"){
        cloudAccessLimited=true; cloudAccessMessage="此帳號沒有執行這項雲端寫入的權限。"; cloudStatus="connected";
      }else{
        cloudAccessLimited=false; cloudAccessMessage=""; cloudStatus="error"; queueCloudSyncRetry(snapshot.id, snapshot.cloudCode);
      }
      return false;
    }
  }

  const localOk = await localSavePromise;
  return localOk; // no cloud room active; report the real local persistence result
}

async function ensureCloudAvailableForArchive(){
  if(cloudAvailable()) return true;
  if(!(typeof window!=="undefined" && window.cloudSync)) return false;
  try{
    if(typeof window.cloudSync.connect==="function") await window.cloudSync.connect();
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("[archive] cloud reconnect failed", e);
  }
  return cloudAvailable();
}

async function writeArchiveStateToCloud(snapshot){
  if(!snapshot || !snapshot.cloudCode) return false;
  const ready = await ensureCloudAvailableForArchive();
  if(!ready || !window.cloudSync || typeof window.cloudSync.pushUpdate!=="function") return false;
  try{
    await flushCloudStateWrites();
    return await window.cloudSync.pushUpdate(snapshot.cloudCode, snapshot) === true;
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("[archive] direct cloud fallback failed", e);
    return false;
  }
}

// Idempotent: safe to call multiple times for the same tournament (re-opened celebration
// screen, page refresh, remote-state re-application) — only performs the archive write once.
async function performAutoArchive(){
  if(state.archiveStatus==="completed" && state.archivedAt) return true;

  state.settlementAttempt=Number(state.settlementAttempt||0)+1;
  state.settlementPhase="processing";
  state.settlementStartedAt=state.settlementStartedAt||Date.now();
  state.settlementError=null;
  state.settlementErrorCode=null;
  state.updatedAt=Date.now();
  try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}

  // Tester ranked rooms use a completely isolated test ladder. Official ladderMode stays general.
  if(isOwnTestTournament() && state.testLadderEnabled===true){
    state.settlementPhase="ladder";
    try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
    if(!state.cloudCode || !cloudAvailable() || !window.cloudSync || !window.cloudSync.settleTestLadderTournament){
      showToast("測試積分賽必須連上 Firebase 才能完成測試天梯結算", true);
      return false;
    }
    let payload;
    try{ payload=buildLadderSettlementPayload(state); }
    catch(e){ showToast(e.message||'V1 積分資料驗證失敗',true); return false; }
    const requiredTop=Math.min(4,payload.participants.length);
    const topCount=payload.participants.filter(x=>x.placement&&x.placement<=4).length;
    if(topCount<requiredTop){ showToast("測試天梯結算失敗：前四名資料尚未完整。",true); return false; }
    try{
      await flushCloudStateWrites();
      const settled=await window.cloudSync.settleTestLadderTournament(state.cloudCode,payload);
      if(!settled||!settled.ok){ state.settlementError=(settled&&settled.message)||"測試天梯積分寫入失敗";await saveRecord(state);showToast(state.settlementError,true); return false; }
      state.testLadderPointsAwarded=settled.skipped!==true;
      state.testLadderSettlementStatus=settled.skipped?"skipped":"awarded";
      state.testLadderSettlementNote=settled.message||"";
      state.testLadderSkippedPlayers=settled.skippedPlayers||[];
      state.testLadderSeasonId="TEST-S1";
      state.testLadderAwardedAt=settled.awardedAt||Date.now();
      state.testLadderResults=settled.results||[];
      showToast(settled.skipped?settled.message:settled.alreadyAwarded?"本場測試天梯先前已完成結算，不會重複加分":"測試天梯積分結算完成");
    }catch(e){ console.error("[test ladder settlement]",e); state.settlementError="測試天梯結算失敗；賽事尚未封存，請確認連線後重試";await saveRecord(state);showToast(state.settlementError,true); return false; }
  }

  // Ranked events are not considered completed until the database-backed
  // ladder transaction has succeeded (or the cloud proves it was already awarded).
  if(state.meta && state.meta.ladderMode==="ranked"){
    state.settlementPhase="ladder";
    try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
    if(!isAdminTierOrAbove()){

      state.settlementPhase="failed";
      await saveRecord(state);
      showToast(state.settlementError,true);

    }
  }

  state.settlementPhase="archive";
  try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
  const wasAlreadyIndexed = recordIndex.some(r=>r.id===state.id);
  const previousCompletedAt = state.completedAt || null;
  state.archiveStatus = "completed";
  state.settlementError = null;
  state.settlementCompletedAt = Date.now();

      else {
        queueCloudSyncRetry(state.id, state.cloudCode);
        if(!localResult.recordOk){
          state.archiveStatus="ongoing";
          state.archivedAt=null;
          state.settlementCompletedAt=null;
          state.completedAt=previousCompletedAt;
          state.settlementError="本機與雲端皆未能完成封存";

        }

        state.completedAt=previousCompletedAt;

        return false;
      }

        state.archiveStatus="ongoing"; state.archivedAt=null; state.completedAt=previousCompletedAt;

        return false;
      }
      showToast("已儲存本機，等待雲端同步");
    }
  }

  if(state.cloudCode && cloudAvailable() && window.cloudSync && window.cloudSync.repairTournamentLifecycle){
    try{ await window.cloudSync.repairTournamentLifecycle(state.cloudCode); }catch(e){}
    publicTournamentsCache=null;

  }

    communityHistoryCache=null;
  }
  state.settlementPhase="completed";
  state.settlementErrorCode=null;
  return true;
}

async function performUnarchiveRecord(rid){
  const hasCurrentData = state.players.length>0 || state.matches.length>0;
  if(hasCurrentData && state.id!==rid && state.archiveStatus==="ongoing"){
    state.archiveStatus="paused"; await saveRecord(state);
  }
  const rec = rid===state.id ? state : (await loadRecord(rid));
  if(!rec){ showToast("找不到該筆紀錄", true); return; }

  rec.correctionLog.push({
    action: "unarchive-for-correction",
    before,
    modifiedBy: adminDisplayName || (userProfile&&userProfile.email) || "admin",
    modifiedAt: Date.now()
  });
  rec.archiveStatus="ongoing";
  state = rec;

      <td><span class="badge badge-metal ladder-tier-${LADDER_TIER_INDEX[ladderTierFromPoints(p.seasonPoints)]||0}">${esc(ladderDisplayTier(p))}</span></td>

    </tr>`).join("")}</tbody>
  </table></div>`;
}

let testLadderPanelExpanded = false;
function canReadTestLadder(){ return isTester() || isAdminTierOrAbove(); }
async function loadTestLadder(force=false){
  if(!canReadTestLadder()) return;
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  if(testLadderView.uid!==uid) testLadderView={uid,rows:[],loading:false,loaded:false,error:""};
  const view=testLadderView;
  if(view.loading || (view.loaded&&!force)) return;
  view.loading=true; view.error=""; render();
  try{
    if(!window.cloudSync?.getTestLadderRanking) throw new Error("not-ready");
    const rows=await ladderReadWithTimeout(window.cloudSync.getTestLadderRanking(),10000,"test-ladder");
    if(currentAuthUid()!==uid || !canReadTestLadder() || testLadderView!==view) return;
    view.rows=rows.filter(p=>p.seasonId==="TEST-S1");
  }catch(e){
    view.error="測試天梯讀取失敗，請確認連線後重新整理。";
  }finally{
    view.loading=false; view.loaded=true;
    if(currentAuthUid()===uid && canReadTestLadder() && testLadderView===view) render();
  }
}
function renderTestLadderPanel(){
  if(!canReadTestLadder()) return "";
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  if(testLadderView.uid!==uid) testLadderView={uid,rows:[],loading:false,loaded:false,error:""};
  const v=testLadderView;
  if(testLadderPanelExpanded && !v.loaded && !v.loading) setTimeout(()=>loadTestLadder(),0);
  const body=testLadderPanelExpanded ? `
    <div class="test-ladder-body">
      <div class="btn-row" style="justify-content:flex-end;margin:12px 0 8px;">
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="test-ladder-refresh" ${v.loading?'disabled':''}>${v.loading?'讀取中…':'重新整理測試天梯'}</button>
      </div>
      <p class="hint">64 位實驗玩家專用。此處顯示測試積分，不計入正式 BXH 天梯。</p>
      ${v.error?`<p role="alert">${esc(v.error)}</p>`:""}
      ${v.loading?'<p role="status">正在讀取測試天梯…</p>':!v.rows.length?'<p>尚無測試積分紀錄，完成一場測試積分賽後即可查看。</p>':`<div style="overflow-x:auto"><table aria-label="TEST-S1 排行榜" style="min-width:560px;width:100%"><thead><tr><th>排名</th><th>編號</th><th>姓名</th><th>積分</th><th>參賽場次</th><th>勝</th><th>敗</th></tr></thead><tbody>${v.rows.map((p,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td>${esc(p.testPlayerKey)}</td><td>${esc(p.playerName||p.testPlayerKey)}</td><td><strong>${Number(p.seasonPoints||0)} PT</strong></td><td>${Number(p.totalEvents||0)}</td><td>${Number(p.wins||0)}</td><td>${Number(p.losses||0)}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`}
      <p class="hint">排序：測試積分 → 勝場 → T 編號。手機可左右滑動表格。</p>
    </div>` : "";
  return `<section class="panel test-ladder-panel" aria-label="測試天梯 TEST-S1">
    <button type="button" class="test-ladder-toggle" data-action="test-ladder-toggle" aria-expanded="${testLadderPanelExpanded?'true':'false'}">
      <span>🧪 測試天梯｜TEST-S1</span>
      <span class="hint">${testLadderPanelExpanded?'收合':'展開'}</span>
      <span class="chev" aria-hidden="true">⌄</span>
    </button>
    ${body}
  </section>`;
}

function ladderSeasonDashboard(control){
  const startAt=Number(control&&control.startAt||0);
  const endAt=Number(control&&control.endAt||0);
  const now=Date.now();
  const dayMs=86400000;
  const seasonId=(control&&control.currentSeason)||"S1";
  if(!startAt||!endAt||endAt<=startAt){
    return {label:"賽季狀態",value:"尚未設定",note:"等待賽季時間設定",state:"pre",progress:0,progressText:"尚未開始"};
  }
  if(now<startAt){
    const days=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((startAt-now)/dayMs));
    return {label:"距離開始",value:days+" 天",note:seasonId+" 尚未開始",state:"pre",progress:0,progressText:"賽季尚未開始"};
  }
  if(now>endAt){
    return {label:"賽季狀態",value:"已結束",note:"等待下一賽季開啟",state:"ended",progress:100,progressText:"本季已結束"};
  }
  const remaining=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((endAt-now)/dayMs));
  const total=endAt-startAt;
  const progress=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(((now-startAt)/total)*100)));
  if(remaining<=7){
    return {label:"賽季倒數",value:remaining+" 天",note:"把握最後積分機會",state:"urgent",progress,progressText:"賽季進度 "+progress+"%"};
  }
  return {label:"本季剩餘",value:remaining+" 天",note:"賽季進行中",state:"live",progress,progressText:"賽季進度 "+progress+"%"};
}

    ${ladderLocationFilterHtml()}
    ${ladderRulesHtml()}
    <div class="panel">
      <div class="panel-title">${esc((control&&control.currentSeason)||"S1")}｜${esc(ladderLocationLabel())}</div>
      ${ladderPublicLoading?'<div class="empty-state">正在讀取天梯資料…</div>':ladderLeaderboardTable(rows,adminMode)}
    </div>
    ${adminMode&&adjustment?`<div class="panel" style="border-color:var(--gold-dim);">

      <div class="grid grid-2"><div class="stat-box"><div class="label">目前賽季積分</div><div class="value small">${Number(adjustment.seasonPoints||0)} PT</div></div><div class="stat-box"><div class="label">目前生涯積分</div><div class="value small">${Number(adjustment.careerPoints||0)} PT</div></div></div>
      <div class="field" style="margin-top:12px;"><label>更正後賽季積分</label><input id="ladder-adjust-points" type="number" min="0" value="${Number(adjustment.seasonPoints||0)}"></div>
      <div class="field"><label>更正原因（必填）</label><textarea id="ladder-adjust-reason" style="min-height:90px;" placeholder="例如：S1 第 12 場名次更正"></textarea></div>

    </div>`:""}
    ${adminMode&&selected?`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-title">${esc(selected.playerName||"")}｜賽季歷史</div>${renderLadderSeasonHistory(selected)}</div>`:""}
    ${adminMode&&isAdminTierOrAbove()?`<div class="panel"><div class="panel-title">最近積分紀錄</div>${renderLadderTransactionList(ladderAdminLogs)}</div>`:""}
  `;
}
function renderLadderSeasonHistory(p){
  const h=p&&p.seasonHistory||{};
  const keys=Object.keys(h).sort((a,b)=>Number(String(b).replace(/\D/g,""))-Number(String(a).replace(/\D/g,"")));
  if(!keys.length) return '<div class="empty-state">尚無已結束賽季紀錄</div>';
  return `<div class="rank-scroll"><table class="rank-table"><thead><tr><th>賽季</th><th>最終積分</th><th>最終階級</th><th>最終排名</th></tr></thead><tbody>${keys.map(k=>{const x=h[k]||{};return `<tr><td>${esc(k)}</td><td>${Number(x.finalPoints||0)} PT</td><td>${esc(x.finalRankTier||"—")}</td><td>${x.finalRanking?`NO.${x.finalRanking}`:"—"}</td></tr>`;}).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`;
}

function ladderLeaderboardTable(rows, adminMode=false){
  if(!rows.length) return '<div class="empty-state"><strong>排行尚未產生</strong><br>目前尚無玩家取得賽季積分。完成第一場積分賽後，排名將自動顯示。</div>';

    <tbody>${rows.map(p=>`<tr>
      <td class="rank-num">NO.${p.__rank}</td>
      <td class="rank-name"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="card-open" data-uid="${esc(p.uid)}">${esc(p.playerName||p.publicName||"—")}</button>${p.equippedBadge?`<span class="title-chip rarity-${esc(p.equippedBadge.rarity||'common')}" style="max-width:130px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;vertical-align:middle" title="${esc(p.equippedBadge.name)}">${esc(p.equippedBadge.name)}</span>`:""}</td>
      <td><span class="badge badge-metal ladder-tier-${LADDER_TIER_INDEX[ladderTierFromPoints(p.seasonPoints)]||0}">${esc(ladderDisplayTier(p))}</span></td>
      <td><strong>${Number(p.seasonPoints||0)} PT</strong></td>
      <td>${Number(p.careerPoints||0)} PT</td>
      <td>${Number(p.totalEvents||0)}</td>
      <td>${Number(p.championCount||0)}</td>
      ${adminMode?`<td><div class="btn-row" style="gap:5px;"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="ladder-player-history" data-uid="${esc(p.uid)}">賽季歷史</button>${isAdminTierOrAbove()?`<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="ladder-adjust-select" data-uid="${esc(p.uid)}">更正積分</button>`:""}</div></td>`:""}
    </tr>`).join("")}</tbody>
  </table></div>`;
}
let testLadderView = {uid:null,rows:[],loading:false,loaded:false,error:""};
let testLadderPanelExpanded = false;
function canReadTestLadder(){ return isTester() || isAdminTierOrAbove(); }
async function loadTestLadder(force=false){
  if(!canReadTestLadder()) return;
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  if(testLadderView.uid!==uid) testLadderView={uid,rows:[],loading:false,loaded:false,error:""};
  const view=testLadderView;
  if(view.loading || (view.loaded&&!force)) return;
  view.loading=true; view.error=""; render();
  try{
    if(!window.cloudSync?.getTestLadderRanking) throw new Error("not-ready");
    const rows=await ladderReadWithTimeout(window.cloudSync.getTestLadderRanking(),10000,"test-ladder");
    if(currentAuthUid()!==uid || !canReadTestLadder() || testLadderView!==view) return;
    view.rows=rows.filter(p=>p.seasonId==="TEST-S1");
  }catch(e){
    view.error="測試天梯讀取失敗，請確認連線後重新整理。";
  }finally{
    view.loading=false; view.loaded=true;
    if(currentAuthUid()===uid && canReadTestLadder() && testLadderView===view) render();
  }
}
function renderTestLadderPanel(){
  if(!canReadTestLadder()) return "";
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  if(testLadderView.uid!==uid) testLadderView={uid,rows:[],loading:false,loaded:false,error:""};
  const v=testLadderView;
  if(testLadderPanelExpanded && !v.loaded && !v.loading) setTimeout(()=>loadTestLadder(),0);
  const body=testLadderPanelExpanded ? `
    <div class="test-ladder-body">
      <div class="btn-row" style="justify-content:flex-end;margin:12px 0 8px;">
        <button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="test-ladder-refresh" ${v.loading?'disabled':''}>${v.loading?'讀取中…':'重新整理測試天梯'}</button>
      </div>
      <p class="hint">64 位實驗玩家專用。此處顯示測試積分，不計入正式 BXH 天梯。</p>
      ${v.error?`<p role="alert">${esc(v.error)}</p>`:""}
      ${v.loading?'<p role="status">正在讀取測試天梯…</p>':!v.rows.length?'<p>尚無測試積分紀錄，完成一場測試積分賽後即可查看。</p>':`<div style="overflow-x:auto"><table aria-label="TEST-S1 排行榜" style="min-width:560px;width:100%"><thead><tr><th>排名</th><th>編號</th><th>姓名</th><th>積分</th><th>參賽場次</th><th>勝</th><th>敗</th></tr></thead><tbody>${v.rows.map((p,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td>${esc(p.testPlayerKey)}</td><td>${esc(p.playerName||p.testPlayerKey)}</td><td><strong>${Number(p.seasonPoints||0)} PT</strong></td><td>${Number(p.totalEvents||0)}</td><td>${Number(p.wins||0)}</td><td>${Number(p.losses||0)}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`}
      <p class="hint">排序：測試積分 → 勝場 → T 編號。手機可左右滑動表格。</p>
    </div>` : "";
  return `<section class="panel test-ladder-panel" aria-label="測試天梯 TEST-S1">
    <button type="button" class="test-ladder-toggle" data-action="test-ladder-toggle" aria-expanded="${testLadderPanelExpanded?'true':'false'}">
      <span>🧪 測試天梯｜TEST-S1</span>
      <span class="hint">${testLadderPanelExpanded?'收合':'展開'}</span>
      <span class="chev" aria-hidden="true">⌄</span>
    </button>
    ${body}
  </section>`;
}

function ladderSeasonDashboard(control){
  const startAt=Number(control&&control.startAt||0);
  const endAt=Number(control&&control.endAt||0);
  const now=Date.now();
  const dayMs=86400000;
  const seasonId=(control&&control.currentSeason)||"S1";
  if(!startAt||!endAt||endAt<=startAt){
    return {label:"賽季狀態",value:"尚未設定",note:"等待賽季時間設定",state:"pre",progress:0,progressText:"尚未開始"};
  }
  if(now<startAt){
    const days=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((startAt-now)/dayMs));
    return {label:"距離開始",value:days+" 天",note:seasonId+" 尚未開始",state:"pre",progress:0,progressText:"賽季尚未開始"};
  }
  if(now>endAt){
    return {label:"賽季狀態",value:"已結束",note:"等待下一賽季開啟",state:"ended",progress:100,progressText:"本季已結束"};
  }
  const remaining=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((endAt-now)/dayMs));
  const total=endAt-startAt;
  const progress=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(((now-startAt)/total)*100)));
  if(remaining<=7){
    return {label:"賽季倒數",value:remaining+" 天",note:"把握最後積分機會",state:"urgent",progress,progressText:"賽季進度 "+progress+"%"};
  }
  return {label:"本季剩餘",value:remaining+" 天",note:"賽季進行中",state:"live",progress,progressText:"賽季進度 "+progress+"%"};
}

}

      ${adminMode?`<td><div class="btn-row" style="gap:5px;"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="ladder-player-history" data-uid="${esc(p.uid)}">賽季歷史</button>${isAdminTierOrAbove()?`<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="ladder-adjust-select" data-uid="${esc(p.uid)}">更正積分</button>`:""}</div></td>`:""}
    </tr>`).join("")}</tbody>
  </table></div>`;
}
let testLadderView = {uid:null,rows:[],loading:false,loaded:false,error:""};
let testLadderPanelExpanded = false;
function canReadTestLadder(){ return isTester() || isAdminTierOrAbove(); }
async function loadTestLadder(force=false){
  if(!canReadTestLadder()) return;
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  if(testLadderView.uid!==uid) testLadderView={uid,rows:[],loading:false,loaded:false,error:""};
  const view=testLadderView;
  if(view.loading || (view.loaded&&!force)) return;
  view.loading=true; view.error=""; render();
  try{
    if(!window.cloudSync?.getTestLadderRanking) throw new Error("not-ready");
    const rows=await ladderReadWithTimeout(window.cloudSync.getTestLadderRanking(),10000,"test-ladder");
    if(currentAuthUid()!==uid || !canReadTestLadder() || testLadderView!==view) return;
    view.rows=rows.filter(p=>p.seasonId==="TEST-S1");
  }catch(e){
    view.error="測試天梯讀取失敗，請確認連線後重新整理。";
  }finally{
    view.loading=false; view.loaded=true;
    if(currentAuthUid()===uid && canReadTestLadder() && testLadderView===view) render();
  }
}
function renderTestLadderPanel(){
  if(!canReadTestLadder()) return "";
  const uid=currentAuthUid();
  if(testLadderView.uid!==uid) testLadderView={uid,rows:[],loading:false,loaded:false,error:""};
  const v=testLadderView;
  if(testLadderPanelExpanded && !v.loaded && !v.loading) setTimeout(()=>loadTestLadder(),0);
  const body=testLadderPanelExpanded ? `
    <div class="test-ladder-body">
      <div class="btn-row" style="justify-content:flex-end;margin:12px 0 8px;">

      </div>
      <p class="hint">64 位實驗玩家專用。此處顯示測試積分，不計入正式 BXH 天梯。</p>
      ${v.error?`<p role="alert">${esc(v.error)}</p>`:""}
      ${v.loading?'<p role="status">正在讀取測試天梯…</p>':!v.rows.length?'<p>尚無測試積分紀錄，完成一場測試積分賽後即可查看。</p>':`<div style="overflow-x:auto"><table aria-label="TEST-S1 排行榜" style="min-width:560px;width:100%"><thead><tr><th>排名</th><th>編號</th><th>姓名</th><th>積分</th><th>參賽場次</th><th>勝</th><th>敗</th></tr></thead><tbody>${v.rows.map((p,i)=>`<tr><td>${i+1}</td><td>${esc(p.testPlayerKey)}</td><td>${esc(p.playerName||p.testPlayerKey)}</td><td><strong>${Number(p.seasonPoints||0)} PT</strong></td><td>${Number(p.totalEvents||0)}</td><td>${Number(p.wins||0)}</td><td>${Number(p.losses||0)}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`}
      <p class="hint">排序：測試積分 → 勝場 → T 編號。手機可左右滑動表格。</p>
    </div>` : "";
  return `<section class="panel test-ladder-panel" aria-label="測試天梯 TEST-S1">

      <span>🧪 測試天梯｜TEST-S1</span>
      <span class="hint">${testLadderPanelExpanded?'收合':'展開'}</span>
      <span class="chev" aria-hidden="true">⌄</span>
    </button>
    ${body}
  </section>`;

function ladderSeasonDashboard(control){
  const startAt=Number(control&&control.startAt||0);
  const endAt=Number(control&&control.endAt||0);
  const now=Date.now();
  const dayMs=86400000;
  const seasonId=(control&&control.currentSeason)||"S1";
  if(!startAt||!endAt||endAt<=startAt){
    return {label:"賽季狀態",value:"尚未設定",note:"等待賽季時間設定",state:"pre",progress:0,progressText:"尚未開始"};
  }
  if(now<startAt){
    const days=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((startAt-now)/dayMs));
    return {label:"距離開始",value:days+" 天",note:seasonId+" 尚未開始",state:"pre",progress:0,progressText:"賽季尚未開始"};
  }
  if(now>endAt){
    return {label:"賽季狀態",value:"已結束",note:"等待下一賽季開啟",state:"ended",progress:100,progressText:"本季已結束"};
  }
  const remaining=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((endAt-now)/dayMs));
  const total=endAt-startAt;
  const progress=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(((now-startAt)/total)*100)));
  if(remaining<=7){
    return {label:"賽季倒數",value:remaining+" 天",note:"把握最後積分機會",state:"urgent",progress,progressText:"賽季進度 "+progress+"%"};
  }
  return {label:"本季剩餘",value:remaining+" 天",note:"賽季進行中",state:"live",progress,progressText:"賽季進度 "+progress+"%"};
}

      </div>
      <div class="hint" style="margin-top:10px;">${isTrialSeason?"S0 為積分體驗版：賽季分與牌位照常計算，但不計入正式生涯積分、最高牌位與永久成就；10/1 00:00 將保存體驗紀錄並由 S1 重新從 0 分開始。":"每 4 個月為一個正式賽季。S1：2026/10/01～2027/01/31。"}<br>同分依序比較：冠軍次數 → 亞軍 → 季軍 → 殿軍 → 生涯積分；仍相同則並列。</div>

}
function renderLadderSeasonHistory(p){
  const h=p&&p.seasonHistory||{};
  const keys=Object.keys(h).sort((a,b)=>Number(String(b).replace(/\D/g,""))-Number(String(a).replace(/\D/g,"")));
  if(!keys.length) return '<div class="empty-state">尚無已結束賽季紀錄</div>';
  return `<div class="rank-scroll"><table class="rank-table"><thead><tr><th>賽季</th><th>最終積分</th><th>最終階級</th><th>最終排名</th></tr></thead><tbody>${keys.map(k=>{const x=h[k]||{};return `<tr><td>${esc(k)}</td><td>${Number(x.finalPoints||0)} PT</td><td>${esc(x.finalRankTier||"—")}</td><td>${x.finalRanking?`NO.${x.finalRanking}`:"—"}</td></tr>`;}).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`;
}
function renderLadderTransactionList(logs){
  if(!logs||!logs.length) return '<div class="empty-state">尚無積分紀錄</div>';
  return `<div class="rank-scroll"><table class="rank-table"><thead><tr><th>時間</th><th>玩家</th><th>類型</th><th>異動</th><th>賽季</th><th>原因／賽事</th><th>操作人</th></tr></thead><tbody>${logs.slice(0,100).map(x=>`<tr><td>${x.createdAtMs?new Date(x.createdAtMs).toLocaleString():"—"}</td><td>${esc(x.playerName||"—")}</td><td>${x.type==="event"?"賽事積分":x.type==="adjustment"?"手動更正":esc(x.type||"—")}</td><td>${Number(x.delta||x.pointsEarned||0)>=0?"+":""}${Number(x.delta||x.pointsEarned||0)}</td><td>${esc(x.seasonId||"—")}</td><td>${esc(x.reason||x.eventName||"—")+(x.scoring?`<br><small>V1｜${Number(x.scoring.effectiveCount)||0} 人 ×${Number(x.scoring.multiplier).toFixed(3)}｜${Number(x.scoring.basePoints)||0}＋${Number(x.scoring.weightedBonus)||0} 分</small>`:"")}</td><td>${esc(x.actorName||x.actorUid||"—")}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`;
}

function deriveDoubleElimPlacements(st){
  const champ=st.championId, runner=st.runnerUpId;
  const elimination=[];
  const lossCounts={};
  (st.matches||[]).filter(m=>!m.isBye&&m.completed&&m.loserId).sort((a,b)=>(a.seq||0)-(b.seq||0)).forEach(m=>{
    lossCounts[m.loserId]=(lossCounts[m.loserId]||0)+1;
    if(lossCounts[m.loserId]>=2) elimination.push({id:m.loserId,seq:Number(m.seq||0)});
  });
  const rest=elimination.filter(x=>x.id!==runner&&x.id!==champ).sort((a,b)=>b.seq-a.seq);
  return { championId:champ, runnerUpId:runner, thirdId:rest[0]&&rest[0].id||null, fourthId:rest[1]&&rest[1].id||null };
}

  let places={championId:st.championId,runnerUpId:st.run
--- match 6 @ 1042752 ---
AuthUid()){c.busy=false;render();}}}
function bindPlayerCardInputs(){document.querySelectorAll('[data-card-privacy]').forEach(el=>{el.onchange=()=>{const c=cardContext();if(c.settings){c.settings[el.dataset.cardPrivacy]=el.checked;if(el.dataset.cardPrivacy==='cardPublic'&&!el.checked){c.settings.searchable=false;const b=document.querySelector('[data-card-privacy="searchable"]');if(b)b.checked=false;}}};});const q=document.getElementById('player-card-query');if(q){q.oninput=()=>cardContext().query=q.value;q.onkeydown=e=>{if(e.key==='Enter'){e.preventDefault();handlePlayerCard('card-search');}};}}

function ladderLeaderboardTable(rows, adminMode=false){
  if(!rows.length) return '<div class="empty-state"><strong>排行尚未產生</strong><br>目前尚無玩家取得賽季積分。完成第一場積分賽後，排名將自動顯示。</div>';
  return `<div class="rank-scroll"><table class="rank-table ladder-rank-table">
    <thead><tr><th>排名</th><th>玩家姓名</th><th>目前階級</th><th>賽季積分</th><th>生涯積分</th><th>參賽場次</th><th>冠軍</th>${adminMode?'<th>管理</th>':''}</tr></thead>
    <tbody>${rows.map(p=>`<tr>
      <td class="rank-num">NO.${p.__rank}</td>
      <td class="rank-name"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="card-open" data-uid="${esc(p.uid)}">${esc(p.playerName||p.publicName||"—")}</button>${p.equippedBadge?`<span class="title-chip rarity-${esc(p.equippedBadge.rarity||'common')}" style="max-width:130px;overflow:hidden;text-overflow:ellipsis;white-space:nowrap;vertical-align:middle" title="${esc(p.equippedBadge.name)}">${esc(p.equippedBadge.name)}</span>`:""}</td>
      <td><span class="badge badge-metal ladder-tier-${LADDER_TIER_INDEX[ladderTierFromPoints(p.seasonPoints)]||0}">${esc(ladderDisplayTier(p))}</span></td>
      <td><strong>${Number(p.seasonPoints||0)} PT</strong></td>
      <td>${Number(p.careerPoints||0)} PT</td>
      <td>${Number(p.totalEvents||0)}</td>
      <td>${Number(p.championCount||0)}</td>
      ${adminMode?`<td><div class="btn-row" style="gap:5px;"><button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="ladder-player-history" data-uid="${esc(p.uid)}">賽季歷史</button>${isAdminTierOrAbove()?`<button class="btn btn-ghost btn-sm" data-action="ladder-adjust-select" data-uid="${esc(p.uid)}">更正積分</button>`:""}</div></td>`:""}
    </tr>`).join("")}</tbody>
  </table></div>`;
}

function ladderSeasonDashboard(control){
  const startAt=Number(control&&control.startAt||0);
  const endAt=Number(control&&control.endAt||0);
  const now=Date.now();
  const dayMs=86400000;
  const seasonId=(control&&control.currentSeason)||"S1";
  if(!startAt||!endAt||endAt<=startAt){
    return {label:"賽季狀態",value:"尚未設定",note:"等待賽季時間設定",state:"pre",progress:0,progressText:"尚未開始"};
  }
  if(now<startAt){
    const days=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((startAt-now)/dayMs));
    return {label:"距離開始",value:days+" 天",note:seasonId+" 尚未開始",state:"pre",progress:0,progressText:"賽季尚未開始"};
  }
  if(now>endAt){
    return {label:"賽季狀態",value:"已結束",note:"等待下一賽季開啟",state:"ended",progress:100,progressText:"本季已結束"};
  }
  const remaining=Math.max(1,Math.ceil((endAt-now)/dayMs));
  const total=endAt-startAt;
  const progress=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,Math.round(((now-startAt)/total)*100)));
  if(remaining<=7){
    return {label:"賽季倒數",value:remaining+" 天",note:"把握最後積分機會",state:"urgent",progress,progressText:"賽季進度 "+progress+"%"};
  }
  return {label:"本季剩餘",value:remaining+" 天",note:"賽季進行中",state:"live",progress,progressText:"賽季進度 "+progress+"%"};
}

function renderLadderLeaderboardPage(adminMode=false){
  if(!ladderPublicLoaded && !ladderPublicLoading) setTimeout(()=>loadLadderPublic(false),0);
  if(adminMode && isAdminTierOrAbove() && !ladderAdminLogsLoaded) setTimeout(()=>loadLadderAdminLogs(false),0);

}
function renderLadderSeasonHistory(p){
  const h=p&&p.seasonHistory||{};
  const keys=Object.keys(h).sort((a,b)=>Number(String(b).replace(/\D/g,""))-Number(String(a).replace(/\D/g,"")));
  if(!keys.length) return '<div class="empty-state">尚無已結束賽季紀錄</div>';
  return `<div class="rank-scroll"><table class="rank-table"><thead><tr><th>賽季</th><th>最終積分</th><th>最終階級</th><th>最終排名</th></tr></thead><tbody>${keys.map(k=>{const x=h[k]||{};return `<tr><td>${esc(k)}</td><td>${Number(x.finalPoints||0)} PT</td><td>${esc(x.finalRankTier||"—")}</td><td>${x.finalRanking?`NO.${x.finalRanking}`:"—"}</td></tr>`;}).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`;
}
function renderLadderTransactionList(logs){
  if(!logs||!logs.length) return '<div class="empty-state">尚無積分紀錄</div>';
  return `<div class="rank-scroll"><table class="rank-table"><thead><tr><th>時間</th><th>玩家</th><th>類型</th><th>異動</th><th>賽季</th><th>原因／賽事</th><th>操作人</th></tr></thead><tbody>${logs.slice(0,100).map(x=>`<tr><td>${x.createdAtMs?new Date(x.createdAtMs).toLocaleString():"—"}</td><td>${esc(x.playerName||"—")}</td><td>${x.type==="event"?"賽事積分":x.type==="adjustment"?"手動更正":esc(x.type||"—")}</td><td>${Number(x.delta||x.pointsEarned||0)>=0?"+":""}${Number(x.delta||x.pointsEarned||0)}</td><td>${esc(x.seasonId||"—")}</td><td>${esc(x.reason||x.eventName||"—")+(x.scoring?`<br><small>V1｜${Number(x.scoring.effectiveCount)||0} 人 ×${Number(x.scoring.multiplier).toFixed(3)}｜${Number(x.scoring.basePoints)||0}＋${Number(x.scoring.weightedBonus)||0} 分</small>`:"")}</td><td>${esc(x.actorName||x.actorUid||"—")}</td></tr>`).join("")}</tbody></table></div>`;
}

function deriveDoubleElimPlacements(st){
  const champ=st.championId, runner=st.runnerUpId;
  const elimination=[];
  const lossCounts={};
  (st.matches||[]).filter(m=>!m.isBye&&m.completed&&m.loserId).sort((a,b)=>(a.seq||0)-(b.seq||0)).forEach(m=>{
    lossCounts[m.loserId]=(lossCounts[m.loserId]||0)+1;
    if(lossCounts[m.loserId]>=2) elimination.push({id:m.loserId,seq:Number(m.seq||0)});
  });
  const rest=elimination.filter(x=>x.id!==runner&&x.id!==champ).sort((a,b)=>b.seq-a.seq);
  return { championId:champ, runnerUpId:runner, thirdId:rest[0]&&rest[0].id||null, fourthId:rest[1]&&rest[1].id||null };
}

    async getLadderSnapshot(){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return {control:null,season:null,players:[]};

      // Control and player collection are independent: fetch them in parallel.
      // Previously these were serial, followed by another season read, causing
      // avoidable latency on mobile even with only one ladder player.

        fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSystem","current")),
        fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers"))
      ]);
      const control=controlSnap.exists()?controlSnap.data():null;
      const players=[]; ps.forEach(d=>players.push(Object.assign({uid:d.id},d.data())));

      let season=null;
      if(control&&control.currentSeason){
        const ss=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSeasons",control.currentSeason));
        if(ss.exists()) season=ss.data();
      }
      return {control,season,players};
    },

    async adjustLadderPoints(uid,newSeasonPoints,reason,actorName){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!uid||!reason) return {ok:false,reason:"invalid"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;

        return {ok:true,before,after,delta};
      });
    },

    async openNewLadderSeason(actorName){

    async getPlayerBadges(uids){const call=fnx.httpsCallable(functionsHandle,'playerCardService',{timeout:15000});const r=await call({action:'badges',uids});return r.data;},
    async getLadderSnapshot(){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return {control:null,season:null,players:[]};

      // Control and player collection are independent: fetch them in parallel.
      // Previously these were serial, followed by another season read, causing
      // avoidable latency on mobile even with only one ladder player.
      const [controlSnap,ps]=await Promise.all([
        fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSystem","current")),
        fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers"))
      ]);
      const control=controlSnap.exists()?controlSnap.data():null;
      const players=[]; ps.forEach(d=>players.push(Object.assign({uid:d.id},d.data())));

      let season=null;
      if(control&&control.currentSeason){
        const ss=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSeasons",control.currentSeason));
        if(ss.exists()) season=ss.data();
      }
      return {control,season,players};
    },

      return s.exists()?Object.assign({uid:s.id},s.data()):null;
    },

      return out.sort((a,b)=>(b.createdAtMs||0)-(a.createdAtMs||0)).slice(0,200);
    },

    async settleTestLadderTournament(code,payload){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!code) return {ok:false,reason:"network"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      const eventCode=String(code).toUpperCase();
      const tournamentSnap=await fx.getDoc(fx.doc(dbHandle,"tournaments",eventCode));
      if(!tournamentSnap.exists()) return {ok:false,reason:"event-not-found"};
      const tournament=tournamentSnap.data()||{};
      if(tournament.testMode!==true||tournament.eventAuthority!=="test"||tournament.ownerUid!==actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"not-own-test-event",message:"只能結算自己建立的測試賽事"};
      const items=(payload&&payload.participants||[]).filter(x=>x&&x.source==="test"&&/^T\d{3}$/.test(String(x.testPlayerKey||"")));
      if(!items.length) return {ok:true,skipped:true,reason:"no-test-players",message:"本場皆為現場新增選手，未計入 TEST-S1；僅 T001～T064 實驗玩家可累積測試積分",results:[],skippedPlayers:(payload?.participants||[]).map(p=>p.playerName)};
      const results=[]; let newWrites=0;
      for(const item of items){
        const key=String(item.testPlayerKey);
        const txId=eventCode+"_"+key;
        const txRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"testLadderTransactions",txId);
        const playerRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"testLadderPlayers",key);
        const r=await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
          const prior=await tx.get(txRef);
          if(prior.exists()) return {already:true,points:Number(prior.data().delta||0)};
          const ps=await tx.get(playerRef);
          const old=ps.exists()?ps.data():{};
          const delta=Number(item.pointsEarned||0);
          const next={
            testPlayerKey:key, playerName:String(item.playerName||key), seasonId:"TEST-S1",
            seasonPoints:Number(old.seasonPoints||0)+delta, careerPoints:Number(old.careerPoints||0)+delta,
            totalEvents:Number(old.totalEvents||0)+1, wins:Number(old.wins||0)+Number(item.wins||0), losses:Number(old.losses||0)+Number(item.losses||0),
            championCount:Number(old.championCount||0)+(item.placement===1?1:0), runnerUpCount:Number(old.runnerUpCount||0)+(item.placement===2?1:0),
            thirdPlaceCount:Number(old.thirdPlaceCount||0)+(item.placement===3?1:0), fourthPlaceCount:Number(old.fourthPlaceCount||0)+(item.placement===4?1:0),
            updatedAt:Date.now()
          };
          tx.set(playerRef,next,{merge:true});
          tx.set(txRef,{type:"event",eventAuthority:"test",actorUid,testPlayerKey:key,playerName:String(item.playerName||key),seasonId:"TEST-S1",delta,eventCode,eventName:String(payload.eventName||""),placement:item.placement||null,wins:Number(item.wins||0),losses:Number(item.losses||0),createdAt:Date.now()});
          return {already:false,points:delta};
        });
        if(!r.already) newWrites++;
        results.push({testPlayerKey:key,playerName:item.playerName,pointsEarned:r.points,placement:item.placement||null,alreadyAwarded:!!r.already});
      }
      return {ok:true,seasonId:"TEST-S1",awardedAt:Date.now(),alreadyAwarded:newWrites===0,results};
    },

    async settleLadderTournament(code,payload){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!code) return {ok:false,reason:"network"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      if(!window.engagementService||typeof window.engagementService.settleLadder!=="function") throw new Error("service-unavailable");
      return window.engagementService.settleLadder({code:String(code).toUpperCase()});

    },

    async adjustLadderPoints(uid,newSeasonPoints,reason,actorName){
      if(!cloudEnabled||!uid||!reason) return {ok:false,reason:"invalid"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      const playerRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers",uid);
      const controlRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSystem","current");
      const logRef=fx.doc(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderTransactions"));
      return await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
        const ps=await tx.get(playerRef), cs=await tx.get(controlRef);
        if(!ps.exists()) return {ok:false,reason:"not-found"};
        if(!cs.exists()||cs.data().rolloverInProgress===true) return {ok:false,reason:"season-unavailable"};
        const control=cs.data(); const isTrialSeason=String(control.currentSeason||"").toUpperCase()==="S0"||control.isTrialSeason===true;
        const old=ps.data(); const before=Math.max(0,Number(old.seasonPoints||0)); const after=Math.max(0,Number(newSeasonPoints)||0); const delta=after-before;
        const beforeCareer=Math.max(0,Number(old.careerPoints||0)); const afterCareer=isTrialSeason?beforeCareer:Math.max(0,beforeCareer+delta);
        const rankTier=cloudLadderTier(after); const oldHigh=old.highestRankTier||"黑鐵"; const highest=isTrialSeason?oldHigh:(cloudLadderTierIndex(rankTier)>cloudLadderTierIndex(oldHigh)?rankTier:oldHigh);
        tx.update(playerRef,{seasonPoints:after,careerPoints:afterCareer,rankTier,highestRankTier:highest,lastPointsUpdatedAt:fx.serverTimestamp()});
        tx.set(logRef,{type:"adjustment",playerUid:uid,playerName:old.playerName||"",seasonId:old.currentSeason||control.currentSeason||"S1",isTrialSeason,countsTowardCareer:!isTrialSeason,beforeSeasonPoints:before,afterSeasonPoints:after,beforeCareerPoints:beforeCareer,afterCareerPoints:afterCareer,delta,reason,actorUid,actorName:actorName||"",createdAt:fx.serverTimestamp()});
        return {ok:true,before,after,delta};
      });
    },

    async openNewLadderSeason(actorName){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return {ok:false,reason:"network"};
      const actorUid=authHandle&&authHandle.currentUser&&authHandle.currentUser.uid;
      if(!actorUid) return {ok:false,reason:"auth-required"};
      const controlRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSystem","current");

      // Lock the ladder first. Event settlement checks this flag inside its own transaction.
      const lock=await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
        const cs=await tx.get(controlRef); if(!cs.exists()) return {ok:false,reason:"not-initialized"};
        const c=cs.data();
        if(String(c.currentSeason||"").toUpperCase()==="S0") return {ok:false,reason:"s0-auto-cutover",message:"S0 將於 10/1 00:00 自動切換為 S1，不需手動開季"};
        if(c.rolloverInProgress!==true && c.lastRolloverCompletedAtMs && (Date.now()-Number(c.lastRolloverCompletedAtMs)) < 10*60*1000){
          return {ok:true,alreadyCompleted:true,oldSeason:c.lastRolloverFromSeason||null,newSeason:c.currentSeason||"S1"};
        }
        if(c.rolloverInProgress!==true && Number(c.endAt||0) && Date.now()<=Number(c.endAt)){
          return {ok:false,reason:"season-not-ended",endAt:Number(c.endAt)};
        }
        const oldSeason=c.rolloverFromSeason||(c.currentSeason||"S1");
        const newSeason=c.rolloverTargetSeason||cloudNextSeasonId(oldSeason);
        const oldEndAt=Number(c.endAt||0);
        if(c.rolloverInProgress!==true){ tx.update(controlRef,{rolloverInProgress:true,rolloverFromSeason:oldSeason,rolloverTargetSeason:newSeason,updatedAt:fx.serverTimestamp()}); }
        return {ok:true,oldSeason,newSeason,oldEndAt};
      });
      if(!lock.ok) return lock;
      if(lock.alreadyCompleted) return {ok:true,alreadyCompleted:true,oldSeason:lock.oldSeason,newSeason:lock.newSeason};

      const ps=await fx.getDocs(fx.collection(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers"));
      const players=ps.docs.map(d=>Object.assign({uid:d.id},d.data()));
      // On retry, players already rolled use their saved old-season final values.
      const finalRows=players.map(p=>{
        const h=p.seasonHistory&&p.seasonHistory[lock.oldSeason];
        return Object.assign({},p,{__finalPoints:h&&h.finalPoints!=null?Number(h.finalPoints):Number(p.seasonPoints||0)});
      }).sort((a,b)=> (b.__finalPoints-a.__finalPoints) || (Number(b.championCount||0)-Number(a.championCount||0)) || (Number(b.runnerUpCount||0)-Number(a.runnerUpCount||0)) || (Number(b.thirdPlaceCount||0)-Number(a.thirdPlaceCount||0)) || (Number(b.fourthPlaceCount||0)-Number(a.fourthPlaceCount||0)) || (Number(b.careerPoints||0)-Number(a.careerPoints||0)) );
      let lastKey=null,lastRank=0;
      finalRows.forEach((p,i)=>{const k=[p.__finalPoints,p.championCount||0,p.runnerUpCount||0,p.thirdPlaceCount||0,p.fourthPlaceCount||0,p.careerPoints||0].join("|"); if(i===0||k!==lastKey)lastRank=i+1; p.__finalRanking=lastRank; lastKey=k;});
      const byUid=Object.fromEntries(finalRows.map(x=>[x.uid,x]));

      const pending=players.filter(p=>p.lastRolloverFromSeason!==lock.oldSeason);
      for(let offset=0;offset<pending.length;offset+=350){
        const batch=fx.writeBatch(dbHandle);
        for(const p of pending.slice(offset,offset+350)){
          const final=byUid[p.uid]; const finalPoints=Number(final.__finalPoints||0); const history=Object.assign({},p.seasonHistory||{});
          history[lock.oldSeason]={finalPoints,finalRankTier:cloudLadderTier(finalPoints),finalRanking:final.__finalRanking};
          const newPoints=Math.floor(finalPoints*0.5); const newTier=cloudLadderTier(newPoints);
          batch.set(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderPlayers",p.uid),{seasonHistory:history,seasonPoints:newPoints,rankTier:newTier,currentSeason:lock.newSeason,lastRolloverFromSeason:lock.oldSeason,lastPointsUpdatedAt:fx.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true});
        }
        await batch.commit();
      }

      const startAt=Number(lock.oldEndAt||0)+1 || Date.now(),endAt=cloudLadderSeasonEnd(startAt);
      const finishBatch=fx.writeBatch(dbHandle);
      finishBatch.set(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSeasons",lock.oldSeason),{status:"completed",completedAt:fx.serverTimestamp(),completedBy:actorUid,finalPlayerCount:players.length},{merge:true});
      finishBatch.set(fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSeasons",lock.newSeason),{seasonId:lock.newSeason,startAt,endAt,status:"active",seasonLengthMonths:4,createdAt:fx.serverTimestamp(),createdBy:actorUid});
      finishBatch.set(controlRef,{currentSeason:lock.newSeason,startAt,endAt,status:"active",seasonLengthMonths:4,rolloverInProgress:false,rolloverFromSeason:null,rolloverTargetSeason:null,lastRolloverFromSeason:lock.oldSeason,lastRolloverToSeason:lock.newSeason,lastRolloverCompletedAtMs:now,updatedAt:fx.serverTimestamp()},{merge:true});
      await finishBatch.commit();
      return {ok:true,oldSeason:lock.oldSeason,newSeason:lock.newSeason,startAt:now,endAt};
    },

    // v13.14.4：刪除整場雲端賽事。Firestore 刪除父文件不會自動刪除子集合，
    // 因此先清 registrations / participants，再刪公開鏡像與主賽事文件。
    async deleteTournament(code){

      const normalized = String(code

  cloudSyncQueueTimer = setInterval(async ()=>{
    if(!cloudSyncPending || state.id!==tournamentId || state.cloudCode!==code || !cloudAvailable()){

}

// Builds a copy of the tournament state safe to expose to unauthenticated
// guests / any public reader. Same overall shape as `state` (so the existing
// guest-facing rendering code — live tab, bracket, history — works completely
// unchanged), but with the ONE genuinely sensitive field (`registrations`,
// which stores partial phone numbers for admin duplicate-checking) removed.
// This is what gets written to the separate `publicTournaments/{code}`
// document; the full `state` (including registrations) only ever goes to the
// admin/staff-only `tournaments/{code}` document.
function buildPublicTournamentSnapshot(st){
  return Object.assign({}, st, { registrations: [] });
}

async function saveState(){
  state.updatedAt = Date.now();
  if(state.meta && state.meta.registrationEnabled){
    state.meta.registrationStatus = computeRegistrationStatus(state.meta);
  }
  touchCommunityActivity(state);

  // Snapshot NOW. Later UI mutations must not change the payload of this save.
  const snapshot = cloneStateForSave(state);
  const localSavePromise = saveRecord(snapshot);

  if(snapshot.cloudCode && cloudAvailable() && currentRole!=="guest"){
    try{
      const cloudPromise = enqueueCloudStateWrite(snapshot.cloudCode, snapshot);
      const localOk = await localSavePromise;
      const ok = await cloudPromise;
      if(ok){
        cloudSyncPending = false;
        cloudStatus = "connected";
        cloudLastSyncAt = Date.now();
        return localOk ? true : false;
      }
      const lastCode = (typeof window!=="undefined" && window.__BXH_LAST_CLOUD_ERROR_CODE) || "";
      if(lastCode==="permission-denied"){
        cloudAccessLimited = true;
        cloudAccessMessage = "此帳號沒有執行這項雲端寫入的權限。";
        cloudStatus = "connected";
      }else{
        cloudAccessLimited = false; cloudAccessMessage = ""; cloudStatus = "error";
        queueCloudSyncRetry(snapshot.id, snapshot.cloudCode);
      }
      return false;
    }catch(e){
      await localSavePromise;
      const code=(e&&e.code)||"";
      if(code==="permission-denied"){
        cloudAccessLimited=true; cloudAccessMessage="此帳號沒有執行這項雲端寫入的權限。"; cloudStatus="connected";
      }else{
        cloudAccessLimited=false; cloudAccessMessage=""; cloudStatus="error"; queueCloudSyncRetry(snapshot.id, snapshot.cloudCode);
      }
      return false;
    }
  }

  const localOk = await localSavePromise;
  return localOk; // no cloud room active; report the real local persistence result
}

async function ensureCloudAvailableForArchive(){
  if(cloudAvailable()) return true;
  if(!(typeof window!=="undefined" && window.cloudSync)) return false;
  try{
    if(typeof window.cloudSync.connect==="function") await window.cloudSync.connect();
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("[archive] cloud reconnect failed", e);
  }
  return cloudAvailable();
}

async function writeArchiveStateToCloud(snapshot){
  if(!snapshot || !snapshot.cloudCode) return false;
  const ready = await ensureCloudAvailableForArchive();
  if(!ready || !window.cloudSync || typeof window.cloudSync.pushUpdate!=="function") return false;
  try{
    await flushCloudStateWrites();
    return await window.cloudSync.pushUpdate(snapshot.cloudCode, snapshot) === true;
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("[archive] direct cloud fallback failed", e);
    return false;
  }
}

// Idempotent: safe to call multiple times for the same tournament (re-opened celebration
// screen, page refresh, remote-state re-application) — only performs the archive write once.
async function performAutoArchive(){
  if(state.archiveStatus==="completed" && state.archivedAt) return true;

  state.settlementAttempt=Number(state.settlementAttempt||0)+1;
  state.settlementPhase="processing";
  state.settlementStartedAt=state.settlementStartedAt||Date.now();
  state.settlementError=null;
  state.settlementErrorCode=null;
  state.updatedAt=Date.now();
  try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}

  // Tester ranked rooms use a completely isolated test ladder. Official ladderMode stays general.
  if(isOwnTestTournament() && state.testLadderEnabled===true){
    state.settlementPhase="ladder";
    try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
    if(!state.cloudCode || !cloudAvailable() || !window.cloudSync || !window.cloudSync.settleTestLadderTournament){
      showToast("測試積分賽必須連上 Firebase 才能完成測試天梯結算", true);
      return false;
    }
    let payload;
    try{ payload=buildLadderSettlementPayload(state); }
    catch(e){ showToast(e.message||'V1 積分資料驗證失敗',true); return false; }
    const requiredTop=Math.min(4,payload.participants.length);
    const topCount=payload.participants.filter(x=>x.placement&&x.placement<=4).length;
    if(topCount<requiredTop){ showToast("測試天梯結算失敗：前四名資料尚未完整。",true); return false; }
    try{
      await flushCloudStateWrites();
      const settled=await window.cloudSync.settleTestLadderTournament(state.cloudCode,payload);
      if(!settled||!settled.ok){ state.settlementError=(settled&&settled.message)||"測試天梯積分寫入失敗";await saveRecord(state);showToast(state.settlementError,true); return false; }
      state.testLadderPointsAwarded=settled.skipped!==true;
      state.testLadderSettlementStatus=settled.skipped?"skipped":"awarded";
      state.testLadderSettlementNote=settled.message||"";
      state.testLadderSkippedPlayers=settled.skippedPlayers||[];
      state.testLadderSeasonId="TEST-S1";
      state.testLadderAwardedAt=settled.awardedAt||Date.now();
      state.testLadderResults=settled.results||[];
      showToast(settled.skipped?settled.message:settled.alreadyAwarded?"本場測試天梯先前已完成結算，不會重複加分":"測試天梯積分結算完成");
    }catch(e){ console.error("[test ladder settlement]",e); state.settlementError="測試天梯結算失敗；賽事尚未封存，請確認連線後重試";await saveRecord(state);showToast(state.settlementError,true); return false; }
  }

  // Ranked events are not considered completed until the database-backed
  // ladder transaction has succeeded (or the cloud proves it was already awarded).
  if(state.meta && state.meta.ladderMode==="ranked"){
    state.settlementPhase="ladder";
    try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
    if(!isAdminTierOrAbove()){
      state.settlementError="積分賽事需由管理員／最高管理員完成天梯結算後才能封存";
      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-permission";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);
      return false;
    }
    if(!state.cloudCode || !cloudAvailable() || !window.cloudSync || !window.cloudSync.settleLadderTournament){
      state.settlementError="積分賽事必須連上 Firebase 才能完成；目前未結算，因此不會封存";
      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-cloud-unavailable";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);
      return false;
    }
    let payload;
    try{ payload=buildLadderSettlementPayload(state); }
    catch(e){ showToast(e.message||'V1 積分資料驗證失敗',true); return false; }
    const requiredTop=Math.min(4,payload.participants.length);
    const topCount=payload.participants.filter(x=>x.placement&&x.placement<=4).length;
    if(topCount<requiredTop){
      state.settlementError="天梯結算失敗：前四名資料尚未完整。單淘汰積分賽需完成季殿賽。";
      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-incomplete";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);
      return false;
    }
    try{
      await flushCloudStateWrites();
      const settled=await window.cloudSync.settleLadderTournament(state.cloudCode,payload);
      if(!settled || !settled.ok){
        const missing=settled&&settled.unlinkedPlayers&&settled.unlinkedPlayers.length ? "："+settled.unlinkedPlayers.join("、") : "";
        const msg=(settled&&settled.reason)==="unlinked-players" ? "以下選手無法連結唯一 BXH 玩家帳號"+missing : (settled&&settled.message)||"天梯積分寫入失敗";
        state.settlementError=msg;
        state.settlementErrorCode=(settled&&settled.reason)||"ranked-settlement-failed";
        state.settlementPhase="failed";
        await saveRecord(state);
        showToast(msg,true);
        return false;
      }
      state.ladderPointsAwarded=true;
      state.ladderSeasonId=settled.seasonId||null;
      state.ladderAwardedAt=settled.awardedAt||Date.now();
      state.ladderSkippedPlayers=Array.isArray(settled.skippedPlayers)?settled.skippedPlayers:[];
      state.ladderResults=Array.isArray(settled.results)?settled.results:[];
      ladderPublicLoaded=false; ladderAdminLogsLoaded=false; myLadderProfileCache=null;
      if(settled.alreadyAwarded){
        showToast("本場天梯積分先前已完成結算，不會重複加分");
      }else if(state.ladderSkippedPlayers.length){
        showToast("天梯積分結算完成；"+state.ladderSkippedPlayers.length+" 位測試／未綁定玩家已略過");
      }else{
        showToast("天梯積分結算完成");
      }
    }catch(e){
      console.error("[ladder settlement]",e);
      const raw=String((e&&e.message)||"");
      const known={"admin-required":"只有管理員／最高管理員可以結算正式積分賽","inactive-account":"目前帳號已停用，無法結算","auth-required":"登入狀態已失效，請重新登入","service-unavailable":"積分結算服務尚未部署"};
      const key=Object.keys(known).find(k=>raw.includes(k));
      state.settlementError=key?known[key]:(raw&&raw!=="request-failed"?"天梯結算失敗："+raw:"天梯積分結算失敗；賽事尚未封存");
      state.settlementErrorCode=(e&&e.code)||"ranked-settlement-exception";
      state.settlementPhase="failed";
      await saveRecord(state);
      showToast(state.settlementError,true);
      return false;
    }
  }

  state.settlementPhase="archive";
  try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
  const wasAlreadyIndexed = recordIndex.some(r=>r.id===state.id);
  const previousCompletedAt = state.completedAt || null;
  state.archiveStatus = "completed";
  state.settlementError = null;
  state.settlementCompletedAt = Date.now();
  state.archivedAt = Date.now();
  if(!state.completedAt) state.completedAt = Date.now();
  state.updatedAt = Date.now();

  const hadCloudRoom = !!state.cloudCode;

  if(localResult.recordOk && localResult.indexOk){
    recordIndex = await loadIndex();
    showToast(wasAlreadyIndexed ? "既有賽事紀錄已更新" : "賽事紀錄建立成功");
  }else{

    if(!hadCloudRoom){

    if(!cloudArchiveReady){

}

// Builds a copy of the tournament state safe to expose to unauthenticated
// guests / any public reader. Same overall shape as `state` (so the existing
// guest-facing rendering code — live tab, bracket, history — works completely
// unchanged), but with the ONE genuinely sensitive field (`registrations`,
// which stores partial phone numbers for admin duplicate-checking) removed.
// This is what gets written to the separate `publicTournaments/{code}`
// document; the full `state` (including registrations) only ever goes to the
// admin/staff-only `tournaments/{code}` document.
function buildPublicTournamentSnapshot(st){
  return Object.assign({}, st, { registrations: [] });
}

async function saveState(){
  state.updatedAt = Date.now();
  if(state.meta && state.meta.registrationEnabled){
    state.meta.registrationStatus = computeRegistrationStatus(state.meta);
  }
  touchCommunityActivity(state);

  // Snapshot NOW. Later UI mutations must not change the payload of this save.
  const snapshot = cloneStateForSave(state);
  const localSavePromise = saveRecord(snapshot);

  if(snapshot.cloudCode && cloudAvailable() && currentRole!=="guest"){
    try{
      const cloudPromise = enqueueCloudStateWrite(snapshot.cloudCode, snapshot);
      const localOk = await localSavePromise;
      const ok = await cloudPromise;
      if(ok){
        cloudSyncPending = false;
        cloudStatus = "connected";
        cloudLastSyncAt = Date.now();
        return localOk ? true : false;
      }
      const lastCode = (typeof window!=="undefined" && window.__BXH_LAST_CLOUD_ERROR_CODE) || "";
      if(lastCode==="permission-denied"){
        cloudAccessLimited = true;
        cloudAccessMessage = "此帳號沒有執行這項雲端寫入的權限。";
        cloudStatus = "connected";
      }else{
        cloudAccessLimited = false; cloudAccessMessage = ""; cloudStatus = "error";
        queueCloudSyncRetry(snapshot.id, snapshot.cloudCode);
      }
      return false;
    }catch(e){
      await localSavePromise;
      const code=(e&&e.code)||"";
      if(code==="permission-denied"){
        cloudAccessLimited=true; cloudAccessMessage="此帳號沒有執行這項雲端寫入的權限。"; cloudStatus="connected";
      }else{
        cloudAccessLimited=false; cloudAccessMessage=""; cloudStatus="error"; queueCloudSyncRetry(snapshot.id, snapshot.cloudCode);
      }
      return false;
    }
  }

  const localOk = await localSavePromise;
  return localOk; // no cloud room active; report the real local persistence result
}

async function ensureCloudAvailableForArchive(){
  if(cloudAvailable()) return true;
  if(!(typeof window!=="undefined" && window.cloudSync)) return false;
  try{
    if(typeof window.cloudSync.connect==="function") await window.cloudSync.connect();
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("[archive] cloud reconnect failed", e);
  }
  return cloudAvailable();
}

async function writeArchiveStateToCloud(snapshot){
  if(!snapshot || !snapshot.cloudCode) return false;
  const ready = await ensureCloudAvailableForArchive();
  if(!ready || !window.cloudSync || typeof window.cloudSync.pushUpdate!=="function") return false;
  try{
    await flushCloudStateWrites();
    return await window.cloudSync.pushUpdate(snapshot.cloudCode, snapshot) === true;
  }catch(e){
    console.warn("[archive] direct cloud fallback failed", e);
    return false;
  }
}

// Idempotent: safe to call multiple times for the same tournament (re-opened celebration
// screen, page refresh, remote-state re-application) — only performs the archive write once.
async function performAutoArchive(){
  if(state.archiveStatus==="completed" && state.archivedAt) return true;

  state.settlementAttempt=Number(state.settlementAttempt||0)+1;
  state.settlementPhase="processing";
  state.settlementStartedAt=state.settlementStartedAt||Date.now();
  state.settlementError=null;
  state.settlementErrorCode=null;
  state.updatedAt=Date.now();
  try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}

  // Tester ranked rooms use a completely isolated test ladder. Official ladderMode stays general.
  if(isOwnTestTournament() && state.testLadderEnabled===true){
    state.settlementPhase="ladder";
    try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
    if(!state.cloudCode || !cloudAvailable() || !window.cloudSync || !window.cloudSync.settleTestLadderTournament){
      showToast("測試積分賽必須連上 Firebase 才能完成測試天梯結算", true);
      return false;
    }
    let payload;
    try{ payload=buildLadderSettlementPayload(state); }
    catch(e){ showToast(e.message||'V1 積分資料驗證失敗',true); return false; }
    const requiredTop=Math.min(4,payload.participants.length);
    const topCount=payload.participants.filter(x=>x.placement&&x.placement<=4).length;
    if(topCount<requiredTop){ showToast("測試天梯結算失敗：前四名資料尚未完整。",true); return false; }
    try{
      await flushCloudStateWrites();
      const settled=await window.cloudSync.settleTestLadderTournament(state.cloudCode,payload);
      if(!settled||!settled.ok){ state.settlementError=(settled&&settled.message)||"測試天梯積分寫入失敗";await saveRecord(state);showToast(state.settlementError,true); return false; }
      state.testLadderPointsAwarded=settled.skipped!==true;
      state.testLadderSettlementStatus=settled.skipped?"skipped":"awarded";
      state.testLadderSettlementNote=settled.message||"";
      state.testLadderSkippedPlayers=settled.skippedPlayers||[];
      state.testLadderSeasonId="TEST-S1";
      state.testLadderAwardedAt=settled.awardedAt||Date.now();
      state.testLadderResults=settled.results||[];
      showToast(settled.skipped?settled.message:settled.alreadyAwarded?"本場測試天梯先前已完成結算，不會重複加分":"測試天梯積分結算完成");
    }catch(e){ console.error("[test ladder settlement]",e); state.settlementError="測試天梯結算失敗；賽事尚未封存，請確認連線後重試";await saveRecord(state);showToast(state.settlementError,true); return false; }
  }

  // Ranked events are not considered completed until the database-backed
  // ladder transaction has succeeded (or the cloud proves it was already awarded).
  if(state.meta && state.meta.ladderMode==="ranked"){
    state.settlementPhase="ladder";
    try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
    if(!isAdminTierOrAbove()){
      state.settlementError="積分賽事需由管理員／最高管理員完成天梯結算後才能封存";
      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-permission";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);
      return false;
    }
    if(!state.cloudCode || !cloudAvailable() || !window.cloudSync || !window.cloudSync.settleLadderTournament){
      state.settlementError="積分賽事必須連上 Firebase 才能完成；目前未結算，因此不會封存";
      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-cloud-unavailable";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);
      return false;
    }
    let payload;
    try{ payload=buildLadderSettlementPayload(state); }
    catch(e){ showToast(e.message||'V1 積分資料驗證失敗',true); return false; }
    const requiredTop=Math.min(4,payload.participants.length);
    const topCount=payload.participants.filter(x=>x.placement&&x.placement<=4).length;
    if(topCount<requiredTop){
      state.settlementError="天梯結算失敗：前四名資料尚未完整。單淘汰積分賽需完成季殿賽。";
      state.settlementErrorCode="ranked-incomplete";
      state.settlementPhase="failed"; await saveRecord(state); showToast(state.settlementError, true);
      return false;
    }
    try{
      await flushCloudStateWrites();
      const settled=await window.cloudSync.settleLadderTournament(state.cloudCode,payload);
      if(!settled || !settled.ok){
        const missing=settled&&settled.unlinkedPlayers&&settled.unlinkedPlayers.length ? "："+settled.unlinkedPlayers.join("、") : "";
        const msg=(settled&&settled.reason)==="unlinked-players" ? "以下選手無法連結唯一 BXH 玩家帳號"+missing : (settled&&settled.message)||"天梯積分寫入失敗";
        state.settlementError=msg;
        state.settlementErrorCode=(settled&&settled.reason)||"ranked-settlement-failed";
        state.settlementPhase="failed";
        await saveRecord(state);
        showToast(msg,true);
        return false;
      }
      state.ladderPointsAwarded=true;
      state.ladderSeasonId=settled.seasonId||null;
      state.ladderAwardedAt=settled.awardedAt||Date.now();
      state.ladderSkippedPlayers=Array.isArray(settled.skippedPlayers)?settled.skippedPlayers:[];
      state.ladderResults=Array.isArray(settled.results)?settled.results:[];
      ladderPublicLoaded=false; ladderAdminLogsLoaded=false; myLadderProfileCache=null;
      if(settled.alreadyAwarded){
        showToast("本場天梯積分先前已完成結算，不會重複加分");
      }else if(state.ladderSkippedPlayers.length){
        showToast("天梯積分結算完成；"+state.ladderSkippedPlayers.length+" 位測試／未綁定玩家已略過");
      }else{
        showToast("天梯積分結算完成");
      }
    }catch(e){
      console.error("[ladder settlement]",e);
      const raw=String((e&&e.message)||"");
      const known={"admin-required":"只有管理員／最高管理員可以結算正式積分賽","inactive-account":"目前帳號已停用，無法結算","auth-required":"登入狀態已失效，請重新登入","service-unavailable":"積分結算服務尚未部署"};
      const key=Object.keys(known).find(k=>raw.includes(k));
      state.settlementError=key?known[key]:(raw&&raw!=="request-failed"?"天梯結算失敗："+raw:"天梯積分結算失敗；賽事尚未封存");
      state.settlementErrorCode=(e&&e.code)||"ranked-settlement-exception";
      state.settlementPhase="failed";
      await saveRecord(state);
      showToast(state.settlementError,true);
      return false;
    }
  }

  state.settlementPhase="archive";
  try{ await saveRecord(state); }catch(e){}
  const wasAlreadyIndexed = recordIndex.some(r=>r.id===state.id);
  const previousCompletedAt = state.completedAt || null;
  state.archiveStatus = "completed";
  state.settlementError = null;
  state.settlementCompletedAt = Date.now();
  state.archivedAt = Date.now();

    async ensureLadderSystem(){
      if(!cloudEnabled) return {ok:false,reason:"network"};
      const controlRef=fx.doc(dbHandle,"ladderSystem","current");
      return await fx.runTransaction(dbHandle,async tx=>{
        const snap=await tx.get(controlRef);

        return {ok:true,control:{currentSeason:seasonId,startAt,endAt,status:"active",rolloverInProgress:false,seasonLengthMonths:4},created:true};
      });
